### 6.3 Budget rows (the exit gate's, from v0.2)

| id | quantity | budget | because |
|---|---|---|---|
| `EST-P-1` | the frozen `G-01` value read as a per-component RMS, expressed as the 3-D RMS `S` it would correspond to for residuals isotropic over the three components | 0.0645 m × 1.7321 = **0.1117 m** | `S = √3 ×` the per-component RMS when the three components carry equal mean squares; the registered rule compares `S` with the printed number itself, so a per-component reading of the frozen number would make the rule stricter than its authors' by up to this factor (§8.1's contingency). **RETIRED 2026-10-06 (v0.2), kept visible:** the manager's answer to `EST-Q-001` settles that the frozen numbers are 3-D (§8.1), so the per-component reading is not a contingency; `S/√3` is still printed, beside `S`, and carries no pass or fail. Replaced by `EST-P-2` |
| `EST-P-2` | *(v0.2, replaces `EST-P-1`)* the window the **denominator's** unknown opens: `S` computed with `N − 7` against `S` computed with `N`, for the same residuals | the ratio of the two statistics, squared: 1.0386 × 1.0386 = **1.0787**, which is 96 over 89 to the digits printed (`N` = 96 for a 24-hour day-file on the 15-minute grid, `N − 7` = 89) | the divisor is computed inside the fitting program the frozen number came from, which neither the manager nor this layer opens, and the printed output cannot show it — it is a factor common to the three components (§8.1). If `S_N ≤ F < S_{N−7}`, the fit sits inside this window and §8.1's registered report applies; outside it (`S_{N−7} ≤ F` or `F < S_N`) the denominator does not matter |

## 7. Failure behaviour

*(Step 2's refusals are `EST-F-201` – `EST-F-206`; every one is a returned `EstimationRefusal` carrying its identifier, a message that names the offending values, the parameter where there is one, the number the reason rests on and whatever condition report could be computed. The one hatch is `EST-R-209`.)*

| id | condition | diagnostic must name | never instead |
|---|---|---|---|
| `EST-F-201` | a free parameter has **no information**: `N̂ᵢᵢ ≤ 0` (a zero column, or no rows at all) | **every** such parameter, by name | pick a pivot; return zero for it; drop the column |
| `EST-F-202` | **the scaled factorisation breaks down**: a pivot `t` with `!(t > 0)` (`EST-R-204`) | the parameter at which it breaks, the pivot `t`, and the dependency (`EST-R-208`): the three parameters of largest `|zₖ|` by name with their `zₖ`; `κ` is not available and the report says so | factor an unscaled matrix; add a ridge; return a pseudo-inverse; return numbers for the parameters before the break |
| `EST-F-203` | **the solve certifies no digit**: `B ≥ ½` (`EST-R-206`), or the computed scaled cofactor is not finite | `B`, `κ_est`, `κ_up`, the three largest `κᵢ` and the three largest members of the weakest direction, each by name | return the solution with a warning; widen the threshold |
| `EST-F-204` | **invalid input**: a row of the wrong length; a non-finite row entry, residual or `σ`; `σ ≤ 0`; no parameters, an empty or duplicate name; an elimination naming an unknown or repeated parameter, with a non-finite value, eliminating every parameter, or asked of a system built by `from_normal_system`; a system whose sizes disagree | what is wrong and where: the row index, the parameter name, the sizes | accept it and propagate a `NaN`; default a weight |
| `EST-F-205` | **the scaled covariance or `σ̂₀²` is requested with `ν ≤ 0`**, or from a system built without a residual square sum | `ν`, `m`, `n_free`; or that no residual information was supplied | return `σ̂₀² = 0` or `NaN`; substitute the formal covariance |
| `EST-F-206` | **the exact scaling's range**: a free parameter's diagonal is non-finite or outside `[2⁻¹⁰⁰⁰, 2¹⁰⁰⁰]`, or an entry of the normal matrix is non-finite | the parameter and the value | scale approximately; clamp |
| `EST-F-301` | *(step 3)* **the model refuses the starting point** (or answers with a non-finite objective, residual or Jacobian there) | status `refused`, `iterations = 0`, a message that names **the starting point** and the model's own refusal | start from nowhere; run with a made-up residual |
| `EST-F-302` | *(step 3)* **the damping is exhausted**: `μ > mu_max` (10¹⁶) with no trial accepted | status `refused`, a message that names **`mu_max`** and the damping reached | report arrival; keep raising `μ` |
| `EST-F-303` | *(step 3)* **a stop (the step test, or the limit) comes while the latest trial could not be evaluated** (the model refused it, its numbers were not finite, or step 2 refused the damped system) | status `refused`, a message that names **that the latest trial could not be evaluated** and why (or `k_max` for the limit) | `converged_step` at a point where nothing was ever evaluated |
| `EST-F-401` | *(step 4)* **an unknown parameter in a prior**: a name the estimator (or the damped method's call) does not hold; a name listed twice in one block; an RTN block without exactly three names | the block, the name (and, for a count, the number given), at construction or at `add_prior`, **before any row is formed** | skip the name; match by position; constrain the first three |
| `EST-F-402` | *(step 4)* **an invalid centre or `σ`**: a non-finite centre or `σ`, `σ ≤ 0`, or `σ` outside `[2⁻⁵⁰⁰, 2⁵⁰⁰]` (`EST-R-401`) | the block, the parameter, the value and the range | clamp `σ`; take `|σ|`; read `σ = 0` as "fixed" (a hard fix is elimination, `EST-R-209`) |
| `EST-F-403` | *(step 4)* **an RTN basis that is not finite or not orthonormal**: `max |B Bᵀ − I| > 10⁻¹²` | the block, the deviation and the entry | orthonormalise it silently; accept a transposed or scaled matrix |
| `EST-F-404` | *(step 4)* **a model that already carries its own objective** (`LmEvaluation::objective`) wrapped by `with_priors` (`EST-R-404`) | the model's objective value at the point and that a prior cannot be added to it | add the prior's half to it under an unregistered convention; ignore the objective |
| `EST-F-405` | *(step 4)* **conflicting overlapping constraints**: a parameter named in two blocks | the parameter and **both** blocks | combine the two (add their informations); keep the first or the last |
| `EST-F-406` | *(step 4)* **a parameter both constrained and eliminated** (`SolveOptions::eliminate`, `EST-R-209`), at `solve()` | the parameter and its block | drop the prior; drop the elimination; solve with the prior's `σ` as if `σ = 0` |
| `EST-F-501` | *(step 5)* **an invalid confidence level or degrees of freedom**: `p` not finite or not in the open interval `(0, 1)` (also `α` of the tail forms); `ν < 1` where `ν` is read (the estimated mode, `two_f_2_nu_critical`); a non-finite or non-positive stated `σ₀` | the argument and its value and the interval | clamp `p` to `[0, 1]`; return `0` or infinity for `p = 0` or `1`; read `ν ≤ 0` as "large" |
| `EST-F-502` | *(step 5)* **an invalid pair**: a name the `Solution` does not hold; the **same** parameter twice (`i = j`); a parameter **eliminated** (`EST-R-209`: it has no uncertainty, `Q` is exactly zero there) | the names and which of the three it is | return a degenerate ellipse; substitute the first free parameter |
| `EST-F-503` | *(step 5)* **the scaled covariance is requested without `σ̂₀²`**: `ν ≤ 0`, or the solution was built without a residual square sum (`EST-F-205`'s conditions) | `ν`, `m`, `n_free`, or that no residual information was supplied | return the formal covariance in its place; use `σ̂₀² = 1` |
| `EST-F-504` | *(step 5)* **a degenerate 2 × 2 block**: `det C_ij ≤ 0` or not finite (a perfect fit gives `σ̂₀² = 0` and an exactly zero scaled block; two parameters exactly dependent give a singular one) | the pair, the three entries and the determinant | take the pseudo-inverse; add a ridge; return a line |
| `EST-F-505` | *(step 5)* **an invalid layout**: a state of other than six names, an empty or repeated name; or `Solution::names` not equal to the layout's list (state, then force parameters in registry order), element for element | the first position that differs, both names, and the two lengths | reorder the covariance to fit; accept a permutation; pad or truncate |
| `EST-F-901` | *(exit gate, v0.7)* **the data are not whole**: an SP3 epoch the file marks absent or bad (`999999…`), a satellite record missing at an epoch, an epoch count other than 96, epochs not 900 s apart, or a satellite not in the file | the file, the satellite, the epoch index and what is wrong | drop the epoch; interpolate it; fit the rest and report `N − 1` |
| `EST-F-902` | *(exit gate, v0.7)* **a scale parameter that cannot be applied**: a non-finite `c`, or a wrapped force that already consumes the same `ParameterId` | the force and the parameter | wrap anyway; read `c` as 1 |

## 8. Acceptance tests

### 8.1 The exit gate: what "reaches its frozen residual" means — **registered 2026-10-06, before any fit runs** (plan §4 rule 3)

**The frozen rows, as `oracle/cases.tsv` prints them:** `G-01` **0.0645 m** (`validate_sp3.sh 2246 20230220000 G01`), `G-02` **0.0573 m** (`2246 20230230000 G01`), `G-03` **0.1870 m** (`2246 20230220000 G05`), each "GNSS 7-parameter fit residual RMS"; `oracle/ORACLE.md` §6 records them as **cannonball** fits whose seventh parameter is the SRP scale, against IGS final orbits. The stamps are read as the IGS long-name product stamp `YYYY DDD 0000` (GPS week 2246 begins 2023-01-22, day 22): **G01 on 2023-01-22 and on 2023-01-23, G05 on 2023-01-22**.

**The statistic.** For a fit of a 24-hour arc to the IGS file's positions `pᵢ` of the satellite (the file's own epochs `tᵢ` inside the arc, **`N` of them — the 15-minute grid, 96 epochs**; `pᵢ` in the ITRS in metres) with the fitted model's positions `qᵢ(θ̂)` at the same epochs, in the ITRS (the model's GCRS position rotated by the same chain as everywhere in this tree):

> **S = √( (1/N) Σᵢ₌₁..N ‖qᵢ(θ̂) − pᵢ‖² )**, in metres.

**Which components:** all three, as the 3-D Euclidean norm of the position difference at each epoch. **Which epochs:** every epoch of the file's grid inside the arc; **none rejected**, none skipped (an epoch the file marks absent or bad refuses the test rather than being dropped). **Which denominator:** `N`, the number of epochs — **not** `N − 7` and **not** `3N`; no degrees-of-freedom correction. *[v0.2, 2026-10-06, the first text kept: whether the frozen number's own denominator is `N` or `N − 7` is not known and cannot be told from its printed output; `S` with `N − 7` is reported beside `S` with `N`, and the amendment below governs the outcome.]* Reported beside `S`, never instead of it: `S/√3` (the per-component reading), the radial, along-track and cross-track RMS (each `√(mean of that component²)`), the largest epoch residual, `N`, and the fitted parameters.

**How the frozen numbers are used.** They **rank last** (plan §4 rule 2): they are the predecessor's output and detect only gross error — a sign, an axis, a factor of two. The weight of the exit gate rests on its second clause, the joint covariance's correlations reproduced by finite differences, whose checks are independent of the predecessor. **The registered pass rule: a fit reaches its frozen residual iff `S ≤ F`, `F` the row's printed value** — `0.0645`, `0.0573`, `0.1870` — for the cannonball fit, and the same rule for the box-wing fit reading L5's library.

**The prediction, written with its reason before any fit.** `S/F` between **0.6 and 1.1** for each of the three rows: the cannonball physics is that of L4's `Srp`/ECOM, with L2's field, third bodies, tides and relativity, which the predecessor's fit may not have carried in full; a ratio above 1.1 is a finding, brought to the manager unchanged, not tuned away. (`EST-A-900` states the prediction and the comparison separately; the prediction is not a criterion.)

**Two honest limits, registered with the rule.** (1) **The frozen number's own formula is not recorded anywhere in this tree** — the script is not open to this layer — so the reading above is the executor's. If the predecessor's was a per-component, or a degrees-of-freedom-corrected, RMS, then `S ≤ F` is stricter than its authors' by up to √3 (`EST-P-1`). **Contingency, written now so that it is not a post-hoc loosening:** if `S > F` and `S/√3 ≤ F`, the result is reported as "reaches only under the per-component reading" and **brought to the manager unchanged**; the executor does not call it a pass. (2) The seventh parameter and the cannonball's area and mass are *stated in the test* (plan §3.8); how the SRP scale is carried by L4's `Srp` — which consumes no registered parameter (`PHPR-R-005`) — is decided at the step that builds the fit, with its own ruling, **before** the first fit runs.

**Amendment, 2026-10-06 (v0.2): `EST-Q-001` answered by the manager from his own record; the first text above is kept and this block governs where they differ.** The revival's `validate_sp3.sh` runs its fit with a **900 s step, the IGS file's own sampling**, so the epochs of the statistic are the file's own (`N` = 96 for a 24-hour day-file) — as registered above. It prints a "position RMS" with "x / y / z RMS" beside it, and in the recorded runs **the first, squared, equals the sum of the three squared** (0.3432² = 0.2946² + 0.1451² + 0.0997², to four digits). Therefore:

1. **The frozen numbers are 3-D**, as §8.1 reads them. The per-component contingency (limit (1)'s second sentence, `EST-P-1`) is **resolved and retired**: `S/√3` is reported beside `S` and carries no pass or fail.
2. **The denominator, `N` or `N − 7`, is computed inside the fitting program, which neither the manager nor this layer opens**; the printed output cannot show it, because it is a factor common to the three components. **Registered now, before any fit runs:** the test prints **both**
   > **S_N = √( (1/N) Σᵢ ‖qᵢ(θ̂) − pᵢ‖² )** and **S_{N−7} = √( (1/(N − 7)) Σᵢ ‖qᵢ(θ̂) − pᵢ‖² ) = S_N · √(N/(N − 7))**,

   the 7 being the fit's seven parameters (the six of the state and the SRP scale), side by side and beside `F`. The three outcomes, by `F` the row's printed value:

   | outcome | condition | what the test and the report say |
   |---|---|---|
   | **reaches** | `S_{N−7} ≤ F` | the fit reaches its frozen residual **under either denominator** |
   | **reaches only under the `N` reading** | `S_N ≤ F < S_{N−7}` | stated as exactly that, **brought to the manager unchanged**; the executor does not call it a pass |
   | **does not reach** | `F < S_N` | a finding, brought to the manager unchanged; the first-text prediction (0.6 – 1.1) is compared beside it |

   `EST-P-2` is the width of the middle row, 3.86 % of `S_N`. The pass rule of the first text, `S ≤ F` with `S = S_N`, is the middle row's lower edge; **an unqualified "reaches" needs the first row.**
3. Nothing else of the frozen number's formula is recorded: which components (all three) and which epochs (the file's own) are as registered.

| id | what is checked | expected value | source | tolerance | discharges |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `EST-A-900` | *(built; run 1 on 2026-11-27 missed for five of the six fits, see the TREE-15 record)* **the exit gate's fit statistic** (*not built; written when the step that builds the fits opens, and committed before the first fit runs*): the cannonball fits of `G-01`, `G-02`, `G-03` and the box-wing fit, `S` by the formula of §8.1, printed beside `F`, `S ≤ F` for each; the prediction `0.6 ≤ S/F ≤ 1.1` recorded beside the result as a prediction. *(v0.2:)* **both** `S_N` and `S_{N−7}` printed beside `F`, `S/√3` beside them, and the outcome row of §8.1's amendment named for each fit (`S_{N−7} ≤ F` is the unqualified pass) | `S ≤ 0.0645`, `0.0573`, `0.1870` m (first text); `S_{N−7} ≤` the same for an unqualified pass (v0.2) | `CASES` (ranks last); `IGSFIN`; `MGR`; this section | the printed digits | R-900 |

**The exit gate's configuration and second clause, registered 2026-11-27 (v0.7), before any exit-gate code exists (plan §4 rules 3, 5 and 7).** *(not built; L7 exit gate registration before code — the convention of `EST-A-900`: `speccheck` reads the rows, which discharge `EST-R-901` – `-907` and `EST-F-901` – `-902`.)* The rows below **add to** `EST-A-900` and the text of this section; they do not replace any of it. **`EST-A-900`'s `S ≤ F` rule, its prediction `0.6 ≤ S/F ≤ 1.1` (cannonball only), the `S_N` / `S_{N−7}` table and the three outcomes are exactly as registered above.** The discipline is §8.2's: constructions, constants, tolerances and controls are committed **before** the code; the code and its tests are then written and compiled **without running any fit**; the sizing tool of `EST-R-905` is run **first** and its output committed; each group is then run **once, alone, with `--list-tests` checked first and the log kept and hashed**; a criterion that is missed is reported as written and amended only by the manager, dated, beside the first. A figure marked *predicted* is a prediction, not a criterion.

| id | what is checked | expected value | source | tolerance | discharges |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `EST-A-900b` | *(built; run 1 on 2026-11-27 passed every gated assertion, see the TREE-15 record; not re-run)* **the exit gate's fits and what each says about itself.** (a) **the data, whole:** each of the two pinned files read through `odl::io`'s SP3 reader: 96 epochs 900 s apart, `G01` and `G05` present and unflagged at every epoch; **control (must be refused, `EST-F-901`, naming the epoch):** a copy of a file's bytes, made in the test's scratch directory, with one record of the satellite set to `999999.999999`; a copy with one epoch line removed; (b) **the fits:** for each of `G-01`, `G-02`, `G-03`, the **cannonball fit** (§3.9) and the **box-wing fit** (G01: `gps_block_iif()`; G05: `gps_block_iir_m()`), each from the nominal start, once; each prints the configuration of `EST-R-902`, the statistic block of `EST-R-903` (`S_N`, `S_{N−7}`, `S/√3`, radial / along / cross RMS, the largest epoch residual, `N`, the seven parameters with their formal and scaled standard deviations from `JointCovariance`) and the iteration block of `EST-R-904`; **gated:** the status of each is `converged_gradient`, `converged_step` or `stalled` (never `refused`, never `max_iterations`), `N = 96`, and **`S_N(start) / S_N(end) ≥ 10`**; (c) **reported, not gated:** the same cannonball fits at degree 12 and with set B's planets; the perturbed start of §3.9 (**predicted:** the same minimum, `S_N` agreeing to `10⁻³` relative); the cannonball with the nominal `A·C_R/m` doubled and halved (**predicted:** `S_N` unchanged to `10⁻⁶` relative, `c` scaling inversely); the outcome row of §8.1's amendment for each fit | the fits' own printed record | `IGSFIN`; §3.9; `EST-A-900` | the status set and the factor 10 as stated; `N` exact | R-902, R-903, R-904, F-901 |
| `EST-A-901` | *(built; run 1 on 2026-11-27: the 21 pairs and the columns agree, both controls missed, see the TREE-15 record)* **the finite-difference correlations of the seven-parameter fit** (`EST-Q-009`, the second clause of the exit gate). For each cannonball fit: the frozen sizing (`ν`, `F_j`, `h_j`, `ε_j`; `EST-R-905`; its file committed and hashed **before** the fit, the test refusing to run against a different hash); `J_FD` by central differences of the whitened residual vector (seven columns from the model alone); `N_FD = J_FDᵀJ_FD`; `ρ_FD`; **gated, all `21` pairs:** `|ρ_FD − ρ| ≤ tol_ij = max(10⁻⁶, 8·κ_est·δ_FD)` with `ρ` = `JointCovariance::correlation` of the fit's own solve, `κ_est` its `ConditionReport::condition_estimate` and `δ_FD = max_j ε_j / ‖J_FD,j‖` (the relative error of the FD columns, from the frozen sizing); and each column of the fit's own final Jacobian against `J_FD,j` to `8·ε_j/‖J_FD,j‖` relative. **For an arc that crosses the shadow (`EST-R-904`) the comparison is printed and not gated (`EST-Q-013`).** **Controls (must fail):** the SRP column's sign flipped on the covariance side: the largest `|Δρ|` over the six pairs that touch `srp_scale` is `≥ 10·tol_ij` (**predicted:** the scale is correlated with the along-track-sensitive state components at `|ρ| ≥ 0.3`, so `2|ρ| ≥ 0.6`); and the SRP parameter omitted from the fit (a 6-parameter refit): the largest change of the other fifteen correlations is `≥ 10·tol_ij` (**predicted**, not independently derived) | `J_FD` from the model alone | `MEAS62` / `SPEC-forcemodel` §6.2 (the form of `ε(h)`); `EST-R-210` | as stated; the sizing's `ε_j` and the solve's `κ_est` | R-905 |

**TREE-14 record (2026-11-27): the Committee's rulings, `EST-A-903` and the sizing procedure registered before its run.** `EST-Q-010` – `-013` ruled (§10). **`EST-A-903`**, run 1 of `EST-A-903` (`est_a_903_run1.log`, SHA-256 `12e128d54876655cec71951e61e6a0e1352dd1f0aa00f34e2efffa41d3640a54`, exit 42): 76 of 77 assertions passed, **(c) missed as registered** (`|analytic − D(h/2)|∞ = 8896960.3846739493` against the band `40.607166476547718`) — **classified a defect of the test, not a finding**: the test converted the propagated state to metres (`× 1000`) while `SensitivitySolution::column` is in the state's own unit (km, km/s; `sensitivity_tests.cpp` compares in that unit); the control missed by the same factor and the ratio (`miss/1000 ≈ 8.9 × 10³`) matches the column's size. The test was corrected (no registered tolerance moved) and **only that case was run again**: run 2 of `EST-A-903` (`est_a_903_run2.log`, SHA-256 `796bfc99aeb1f6ebc4af4867444e18585c98677255253caa1e61be0a7de78ebb`, exit 0): **all 77 assertions passed**, band `0.040607166738482192`, `|analytic − D(h/2)|∞ = 0.0033839062598417513` (≤ band), the control (`d(a)/d(r)` passed through unscaled) misses by `68814.26805140845` bands (≥ 10³). Both logs are kept in both archive directories (`exit_gate_runs/`).

**The sizing procedure of `tools/exit_gate_fd_sizing.cpp`, registered before it is run (executor-classified, TREE-14; it makes `EST-R-905`'s "scan" concrete).** One residual evaluation is the 288-vector of §3.9 at the start of the cannonball fit, chained propagation at integrator tolerance `10⁻¹²`. **`ν`** = the largest `max|Δr|` over: the start repeated, and each of the seven parameters moved by `10⁻⁶ s₀ⱼ`, with `s₀` = `(1, 1, 1 m; 10⁻³, 10⁻³, 10⁻³ m/s; 10⁻³)`. **The scan:** for each parameter the ladder `sₘ = s₀·4ᵐ`, `m = 0..3`, and at each step the points `±s`, `±2s`: third difference `Δ³ = r(2s) − 2r(s) + 2r(−s) − r(−2s)` → `D₃ = max|Δ³|/(2s³)`, fourth difference `Δ⁴ = r(2s) − 4r(s) + 6r(0) − 4r(−s) + r(−2s)` → `D₄ = max|Δ⁴|/s⁴`; a difference counts as signal when `max|Δ³| ≥ 100ν` (`max|Δ⁴| ≥ 200ν`); **`F₃ⱼ` / `F₄ⱼ` = the largest `D₃` / `D₄` over the ladder entries that are signal**, and if none is, the bound `100ν/(2s_top³)` / `200ν/s_top⁴` (printed `(bound)`), `s_top = 64 s₀`. **`hⱼ = min((3ν/F₃ⱼ)^{1/3}, s_top)`**, `εⱼ = hⱼ²F₃ⱼ/6 + ν/hⱼ`; **second differences:** `h2ⱼ = min((48ν/F₄ⱼ)^{1/4}, s_top)`, `ε2ⱼ = h2ⱼ²F₄ⱼ/12 + 4ν/h2ⱼ²` (the pair block's mixed second differences use the larger of the two parameters' `F₄` and `h2`, as `EST-A-902` needs). `J_FD` at the start by central differences at `hⱼ`; **`δ_FD = maxⱼ εⱼ/‖J_FD,ⱼ‖₂`** (as `EST-A-901` states it; `εⱼ` bounds one component and the norm is over 288 rows); the indicative `κ` = `λ_max/λ_min` of `D N_FD D`, `D = diag(1/‖J_FD,ⱼ‖)`, **printed, never used by the gate** (the gate uses the fit's own `ConditionReport::condition_estimate`). **`b_pair` cannot be evaluated before the fit** (it needs `H_r` at the solution): what is frozen is `h2ⱼ`, `ε2ⱼ`; the test prints `b_pair` before the profile, as `EST-A-902` says. **The shadow check** uses the 96 file epochs' nominal trajectory states and every 10 s of it (cubic Hermite between epochs), `ν` from `odl::shadow::conical`. The cannonball of each row: the box-wing block's **sun-pointing surfaces' total area** (the panels) — **the body-fixed faces are listed and not added** (a stated choice; `c` absorbs `A·C_R/m`, `EST-R-907` (v)) — the block's cited mass, `C_R = 1`. The tool is run **once**; its stdout, its `--write-header` output (`tests/l7_exit/exit_gate_sizing_frozen.hpp`) and their SHA-256 are kept in both archive directories.

**The sizing run (TREE-14, 2026-11-27): run 1 of `exit_gate_fd_sizing`** (`exit_gate_fd_sizing_run1.log`, SHA-256 `b362002507aa8c2290defbf8736a33e7acb3114df17fb9cc03413d431b26997c`, exit 0; the frozen header `tests/l7_exit/exit_gate_sizing_frozen.hpp`, SHA-256 `db43ee56c60edb190750de366bffd6d069d085f10b8a6b761fca4debd3c68054`; no fit run). **Shadow (`EST-Q-013`): all three rows have `ν = 1` throughout** (96 of 96 file epochs and 8 550 samples at 10 s each: minimum `ν = 1`), so `EST-A-901` is gated for `G-01`, `G-02` and `G-03`. **The cannonballs:** `G-01`, `G-02`: area `22.25 m²` (the Block IIF's sun-pointing surface; the six body-fixed faces, `36.26 m²`, not added), mass `1633 kg`, `A·C_R/m = 0.013625229638701776 m²/kg`; `G-03`: `13.92 m²` (body-fixed `25.64 m²` not added), `1080 kg`, `0.012888888888888889 m²/kg`. `S_N` at the nominal start: `42.46703931017317 m` (`G-01`), `39.596225386225335 m` (`G-02`), `22.135302861179373 m` (`G-03`). **`ν` (m):** `0.00012361537665128708`, `0.00012161117047071457`, `9.2413276433944702e-05`. **Finding, reported and not tuned: in every one of the 21 (row, parameter) scans no ladder step reached the signal threshold (`100ν` for the third difference, `200ν` for the fourth), so every `F₃`, `F₄` is the registered fallback bound** (`100ν/(2s_top³)`, `200ν/s_top⁴`; printed `(bound)`) and every `hⱼ` is `(3ν/F₃)^{1/3}` of that bound: positions `h = 25.055152903480732 m`, `h2 = 44.795334548231466 m`; velocities and `srp_scale` `h = 0.025055152903480732`, `h2 = 0.044795334548231466` (the same for the three rows: the fallback depends on `ν` and `s_top` only, and the ratio is `ν`-independent). `εⱼ` (`G-01`): `7.4005960247471151e-06` (positions), `0.0074005960247471147` (velocities, `srp_scale`); `δ_FD = 1.7860642149272963e-05` (`G-01`), `1.7527314418192749e-05` (`G-02`), `1.2677626011087388e-05` (`G-03`); **indicative** `κ₂` of the scaled `N_FD` at the start `824.64347645441603`, `828.01064940272022`, `1124.2205419559368`, so the **indicative** `tol_ij = max(10⁻⁶, 8κδ_FD)` is `0.11782929626947783`, `0.11610242394954751`, `0.11401958067919477` — **far looser than the `10⁻⁶` floor**: the gate's power on the correlations is about `0.1` at these figures, because the third and fourth differences of the residual are below the noise at every scanned step (the model is smooth to better than `10⁻⁶ m` in `Δ³` at the largest step, `64 s₀`). The Committee is asked whether to keep this registered rule (the bound is conservative and honest, and the power is what it is) or to amend it with a larger ladder (a dated amendment beside the first text, then one new run); **nothing was changed.** Every other figure is in the log and the header.

**The Committee's rulings on TREE-14's three questions (2026-11-27, recorded in TREE-15; v0.7a).** **(a)** The fallback-bound sizing (`tests/l7_exit/exit_gate_sizing_frozen.hpp`, SHA-256 `db43ee56c60edb190750de366bffd6d069d085f10b8a6b761fca4debd3c68054`) **is kept as registered**: the ladder is not widened and `exit_gate_fd_sizing` is not re-run; the Committee's reading is that third and fourth differences over `s ≤ 64 s₀` sit below the `100ν` / `200ν` threshold at GPS altitude over the half-arc, so the fallback bound is the honest conservative bound of the registered procedure (`tol_ij ≈ 0.114 – 0.118`, to separate true correlations from the registered controls). **(b)** The cannonball area choice (the Sun-pointing panels' total, `22.25 m²` for `G01`, `13.92 m²` for `G05`; body-fixed faces listed, not added; cited masses `1633 kg` / `1080 kg`; `C_R = 1`) **is approved**. **(c)** **Evaluating `b_pair` at the converged solution is approved** as registered in `EST-R-906` and `EST-A-902`; `(h2ⱼ, ε2ⱼ)` stay frozen in the header.

**The exit-gate test's readings and choices, registered before its first run (executor-classified, TREE-15; `tests/l7_exit/exit_gate_tests.cpp` and `exit_gate_model.hpp`, committed before any fit).** (1) The model of `exit_gate_model.hpp` is the one of the sizing tool (same forces, tolerance `10⁻¹²`, chained propagation from epoch 48); the LM residual is `p − q(θ)` (unit weight, 288 rows) and the Jacobian is `dq/dθ` from the chain of `propagate_with_sensitivities` (ITRS position `= M r`; the `srp_scale` column converted from km). (2) LM runs with `max_iterations = 100` (a cost limit; reaching it is a `max_iterations` status and so a finding). (3) **`EST-A-900`** gates the first text's `S_N ≤ F`; the three outcomes are printed beside; the box-wing fits are the same fit with `Srp` over `gps_block_iif()` / `gps_block_iir_m()`. (4) **`EST-A-900b`**: the `EST-F-901` controls edit the SP3 text **in memory** (the test's scratch copy; no file is written), the 20th record of the satellite set to `999999.999999` and the 30th epoch line removed, and must be refused with an `EST-F-901` text (control 1 naming epoch 19; control 2 is refused at the epoch after the removal, whose spacing is wrong); the six reported variants of (c) are run for **each** row, from the nominal start except the perturbed one. (5) **`EST-A-901`**: `J_FD` at the **fit's solution** with the frozen `hⱼ`; `δ_FD = maxⱼ εⱼ/‖J_FD,ⱼ‖₂` at the solution with the frozen `εⱼ`; `κ_est` of the fit's own final system; `ρ_FD` from the inverse of `J_FDᵀJ_FD`; the **column comparison reads "relative" as `max_k |J − J_FD|/‖J_FD,j‖₂ ≤ 8 εⱼ/‖J_FD,j‖₂`** (a per-component bound, as `εⱼ` is); control 1 flips the SRP column of the analytic Jacobian at the solution and compares its `ρ` with the fit's `JointCovariance::correlation`; control 2 is a six-parameter refit with `srp_scale` held at 1. A row whose fitted trajectory has `ν < 1` at any of its 96 epochs is printed and not gated (`EST-Q-013`). (6) **`EST-A-902`**: the pairs P1, P2 by the rules from the correlation matrix of `J_startᵀJ_start`; the ellipse from the fit's final `Solution`; `H_r` on the pair block by second differences at the frozen `h2` (the nine model calls: `r(±h2ₐ)`, `r(±h2_b)`, the four corners); **`N_s`, `H_s` are the pair's 2 × 2 blocks of `S N S` and `S H S`** (`S = diag(2^{eᵢ})` from `Solution::scale_exponents`) and `|λ|_max` is the larger modulus of the eigenvalues of `N_s⁻¹H_s` (similar to `N_s^(−1/2)H_sN_s^(−1/2)`), `b_pair = 2|λ|_max s`; **the pair is held in the profile by fixing it in the model (a five-parameter LM): `LmOptions` has no elimination hatch**, which is `SolveOptions::eliminate`'s home (`EST-R-209`) and acts on `NormalEquations`; `P = Ω_min − Ω̂` with `Ω = 2F`; `P_lin = σ̂₀² · δᵀC⁻¹δ` with `C` the ellipse's covariance block (so that `P_lin` is `δᵀQ_ij⁻¹δ`). (7) The four cases run as separate processes, each once, selected by its tag (`[900]`, `[900b]`, `[901]`, `[902]`; the tags are exact, the names are not used because `EST-A-900*` would be a union).

**TREE-15 RECORD, the exit gate's run 1 (2026-11-27; each case once, alone, `--list-tests` first — `exit_gate_list_tests.txt`, SHA-256 `e9cc54f87695e3cb5b0a32acbe3fe2ddb04081f0b7dcdc4f55794413bfe27ed7`; nothing tuned, nothing re-run, STOP-ON-MISS).** Logs (both archive directories `exit_gate_runs/`): `est_a_900_run1.log` SHA-256 `378e7ce4cb0970b383200c6c599e4e8b252fc28fe2219249f45cec2bc60d55fe` (exit 42; 1 224 of 1 229 assertions passed, **5 failed**); `est_a_900b_run1.log` `7720e98c4b1b81bce8af55351fa23372ad943c4a15e90fdba55f008a5733f0c6` (exit 0; 4 948 of 4 948); `est_a_901_run1.log` `d1d5780c83d6598619c716b41a41319051beeae94c6c21c71c83bb7195b3bafc` (exit 42; 1 346 of 1 352, **6 failed**); `est_a_902_run1.log` `62127d7a96eceba122548ac26626d4c92bf4b63bac079edb71a618238ba32266` (exit 0; 800 of 800, **but see the defect below**).

**AMENDMENT 1 OF THE L7 EXIT GATE (2026-11-27, `odl Committee`, TREE-16; v0.7b) — recorded beside the first texts, which stay visible; Run 1's logs are kept; `EST-A-900b` and `EST-A-903` are not re-run.** *Ruling 1 (`EST-A-900`).* The six converged fits are the minima of this tree's own force and frame model on the pinned data (invariant to `< 6 × 10⁻⁵` under degree 12, set B and Desai's pole tide, and to `< 6 × 10⁻⁷` from the `99.6 km` perturbed start: Run 1's `EST-A-900b`). Under `plan/PLAN.md` §4 rule 2 and `EST-R-907` (i) – (ii) the Committee accepts Run 1's classifications as **the recorded findings of `EST-A-900`**: `G-01` cannonball `S_N = 0.0622784085 m ≤ F = 0.0645 m < S_{N−7} = 0.0646812075 m` (`S_N/F = 0.965557`) reaches under the `N` reading; the other five (`G-02` cannonball `1.21632 F`, `G-03` cannonball `1.29517 F`, box-wing `G-01` `1.4696 F`, `G-02` `1.86744 F`, `G-03` `1.29205 F`) are model-difference findings inside the gross-error factor of two. **Amended gate:** `G-01` cannonball `S_N ≤ F`; **all six `S_N ≤ 2F`**; each reproduces its Run-1 minimum (`0.0622784085`, `0.0947894259`, `0.0696950657`, `0.107004257`, `0.242196639`, `0.241612829` m) to `10⁻⁶` relative; the outcome row printed beside `F`. *Ruling 2 (`EST-A-901`).* Run 1's pairs agreed to `4.35 × 10⁻⁷` and its columns to `7.94 × 10⁻⁸`; the controls missed `10·tol_ij > 1` because `tol_ij` is set by the `srp_scale` fallback bound (`ε₆/‖J_FD,6‖ ≈ 1.8 × 10⁻⁵`) times `8κ_est`, while centring at the middle epoch decorrelates `srp_scale` from the state (`max_{j<6}|ρ(j, srp_scale)| = 0.1629 – 0.1734`, so the flipped column changes `ρ` by `2·max` and omitting SRP changes the state–state correlations by about `max|ρᵢ,c ρⱼ,c|`). **Amended gate** (the first text's `|ρ_FD − ρ| ≤ tol_ij` and column bound kept, and added): `tol_ij_state = max(10⁻⁶, 8κ_est·max_{j<6} εⱼ/‖J_FD,j‖₂)` for all 21 pairs; **control 1:** the largest `|Δρ|` over the six pairs touching `srp_scale` is `≥ 2 tol_ij` and `≥ 0.9·2·max_{j<6}|ρ(j, srp_scale)|` and `≥ 10³` times the largest pair difference; **control 2:** the largest change over the fifteen state pairs is `≥ 10 tol_ij_state` and `≥ 0.9·max_{i<j<6}|ρ(i, c)ρ(j, c)|` and `≥ 10³` times the largest pair difference. *Ruling 3 (`EST-A-902`, in the manner of L6 step 4's Amendment A1, `PROVENANCE.md` §39.11).* The test's `H_s` used `2^{+e}` where `N_s` is `2^{−e}` (Run 1's `b_pair` of `10¹¹ – 10¹⁵`): **fixed** (`sa = 2^{−eₐ}`, `s_b = 2^{−e_b}`, `exit_gate_tests.cpp`). With the sign right the curvature term `b_curv = 2|λ|_max` is tiny, and the profile's difference of two objective evaluations carries the frozen evaluation noise: `b_noise(s) = 2·√Ω̂·ν_frozen/P_lin(s)`. **Amended gate:** `b_curv` and `b_noise` printed separately before the profile; `|P/P_lin − 1| ≤ b_curv·s + b_noise` at every point; the prediction `b_curv + b_noise(1.0) ≤ 0.1` reported. *Ruling 4 (`EST-A-900b`).* Not re-run; predictions are never adjusted after the run (plan §4 rule 7): the `10⁻⁶` `×2` prediction stays recorded as met in four of six cases and `1.54 × 10⁻⁶` (`G-02`), `1.01 × 10⁻⁶` (`G-03`) in the other two, at the evaluation-noise floor `ν/S_N ≈ 1.7 × 10⁻⁶`.

**`EST-A-902` — its assertions passed, and its bound is defective (a defect of the test's reading, found in the log; nothing re-run).** Pairs by the rules: `G-01` P1 = `(vz, srp_scale)` (`ρ_start = −0.164900176`), P2 = `(x, vz)` (`0.726513651`); `G-02` P1 same (`−0.162947897`), P2 `(x, vz)` (`0.723716311`); `G-03` P1 `(vz, srp_scale)` (`0.173359153`), P2 `(y, vy)` (`0.781306727`). All 96 profile points were evaluated (95 `converged_step`, one `stalled`), all 48 points at `s = 0.5` lie inside the region by the profile, the sign-flipped control is caught for the three P2 pairs (`0.8172489`, `0.814783204`, `0.862413324`) and is not applicable to the three P1 pairs (`|ρ| < 0.5`; the value is printed: `0.386`, `0.381`, `0.409`). **But the printed `b_pair` is `2.9 × 10¹¹` to `5.2 × 10¹⁵` per unit `s`, so `|P/P_lin − 1| ≤ b_pair·s` is vacuous; the measured departure is at most `9.41 × 10⁻⁴` (P2) and `9.08 × 10⁻⁴` (P1) over all 96 points.** **Diagnosis (a hypothesis, not re-run):** the test forms `N_s`, `H_s` as `S N S` and `S H S` with `S = diag(2^{eᵢ})`, whereas `Solution` scales by `2^{−eᵢ}` (`scaled diagonal = diagonal·2^{−2e}`, EST-R-201; `x = 2^{−e} y`): with the wrong sign `H_s` is inflated by `2^{2(eₐ+e_b)}` against `N_s`'s pair block (which is read directly from `scaled_matrix`), which would explain a `|λ|` of `10¹¹ – 10¹⁵`. The registered formula itself is not shown wrong; **the test must be corrected and `EST-A-902` re-run by a Committee ruling** (the registered `b_pair ≤ 0.1` prediction is printed `NOT met` for all six pairs for this reason).

**Not done / open for the Committee:** (1) the five `EST-A-900` misses are findings to be brought unchanged (the frozen numbers rank last, `EST-R-907` (i)); (2) the `EST-A-901` controls' registered threshold against the kept `tol_ij`; (3) the `EST-A-902` correction and its second run; (4) the `EST-A-900b` `×2` prediction at `10⁻⁶`. `L7` stays open: the exit gate's gated criteria did not all pass.
*Fixed before any run, in the test files' headers (executor-classified, TREE-13):* the configuration of §3.9 and the start; the pair rules; `p = 0.95`; the eight points and `s ∈ {0.5, 1}`; the gross-error factor 10; `tol_ij`'s `8·κ_est·δ_FD` and floor `10⁻⁶`; the sizing procedure; the shadow rule. **Not registered here, because nothing yet exists to measure them:** the frozen sizing figures (`ν`, `F_j`, `h_j`, `ε_j`, the second-difference sizing) — they are produced by the sizing tool and **committed before the first fit** — the cannonball's area and mass (`EST-Q-011`), and the mutation testing of the exit gate's code.

### 8.2 Step 2's acceptance tests — **registered 2026-10-06 (v0.3), before any code of the estimator exists** (plan §4 rules 3, 5 and 7)

**The order of work, and what "registered" means here.** The checks below are committed with their expected values and their reasons before the module is written; the module is then written and compiled **without running any group of them**; each group is then run **once, alone, with `--list-tests` checked first and the log kept and hashed** (the lesson of `feedback_unfiltered_runs_consume_gates`), from the committed tree, and a result that misses a criterion is reported **with the criterion as written**: **a breached bound is a finding, never slack** (the manager, 2026-10-06). Groups: `[scale]` (`EST-A-201`, `-202`), `[kernel]` (`-203`, `-204`, `-205`), `[nist]` (`-206`, `-207`, `-208`), `[decision]` (`-209`, `-212`, `-213`), `[hatch]` (`-210`, `-211`), `[control]` (`-214`, `-215`).

**The fixture.** *The recipe for the NIST rows*, identical in `tools/estimation_sizing.cpp` and in the test, so that every registered figure applies to the very rows fed to the estimator: the data block of the pinned file is read as decimal strings; `strtold` parses each into x87 long double; the design row is `(1, x)` for Norris, `(1, x, x²)` for Pontius, `(x)` for NoInt1 and NoInt2, `(1, x₁ … x₆)` for Longley, `(1, x, …, x¹⁰)` for Filip and `(1, x, …, x⁵)` for Wampler1 – 5, **the powers formed in long double by repeated multiplication (`p ← p·x`), each entry then rounded to double once**; the response likewise; `σ = 1` for every row; rows in file order; parameters named `B0 … Bk` (`B1` for NoInt). **The checksum guard, asserted first for every dataset:** the sum modulo 2⁶⁴ of the bit patterns of every design entry and response, in row order, equals the registered `checksum` of `registered_figures.hpp` — a fixture that drifted fails there and not three assertions later. **Certified values** are read from the same pinned file by the line ranges its header names and parsed by `strtold`; they are quoted below as facts with NIST's citation (§2, `NISTL`). **The extended-precision comparator** of layers (a), (b) is x87 long double with its own error stated (`u′ = 2⁻⁶⁴`, `2⁻¹¹` of `u`: at most 5 × 10⁻⁴ of each bound). **Every data-driven row asserts how many cases it ran and prints the number** (plan §4 rule 3).

| id | what is checked | expected value | source of the expected value | tolerance | discharges |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `EST-A-201` | **the scale rule**: (a) `scale_exponent(d)` for `d` = 1, 2, 3, 4, 8, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 1.99, 7.9, 2⁻⁹⁰⁰, 2⁹⁰⁰, 10⁻³⁰⁰, 10³⁰⁰ is **0, 1, 1, 1, 2, 0, −1, −2, 0, 1, −450, 450, −498, 498** (the scaled diagonals 1, ½, ¾, 1, ½, ½, 1, 1.6, 1.99, 1.975, 1, 1, 0.6697, 1.4932); (b) for 10⁴ values log-uniform over `[2⁻¹⁰⁰⁰, 2¹⁰⁰⁰]` (`std::mt19937_64` seeded 20261006) and at 18 boundary points (`2ʲ` and its two neighbours, `j` ∈ {−1000, −1, 0, 1, 2, 999}) the scaled diagonal `ldexp(d, −2e)` lies in **`[½, 2)`**, exactly; (c) through the estimator: a diagonal of `0` and of `−1` give `EST-F-201`; `2⁻¹⁰⁰¹`, `2¹⁰⁰¹` and a diagonal that overflows to `+∞` (a row of entries 10²⁰⁰) give `EST-F-206`; a `NaN` offered to `from_normal_system` is `EST-F-204` (no `NaN` is reachable through the rows: `add` refuses non-finite values) | the table, from `frexp` and the rule `e = ⌊k/2⌋`, derived by hand in this row and confirmed by the tool before the test exists | the rule (`EST-R-201`), `LAPK` | **0** (integers; the interval bounds exact) | R-201, F-201, F-206 |
| `EST-A-202` | **the scaling is exact, the unscaling is exact**: for 40 problems, each of `n` ∈ {2, …, 9} (`n` seeded), rows = the `n` unit rows followed by `2n` seeded integer rows in `[−8, 8]`, every entry of column `j` multiplied by `2^(pⱼ)`, `pⱼ` seeded in `[−30, 30]` (columns spanning up to 18 decimal orders): the solve **completes**, and `ldexp(N_s,ᵢⱼ, eᵢ+eⱼ) == N̂ᵢⱼ` (from `formed_system()`), `ldexp(b_s,ᵢ, eᵢ) == b̂ᵢ`, `ldexp(yᵢ, −eᵢ) == xᵢ`, `ldexp(Q_s,ᵢⱼ, −(eᵢ+eⱼ)) == Qᵢⱼ` **bitwise**, the scaled diagonal in `[½, 2)`, `Q` **exactly symmetric** | the 40 cases counted and printed; completed **40 of 40** (the unit rows make `N` positive definite before scaling and the scaling cannot hurt it) | `EST-R-201`, `-205` | **0** (bitwise) | R-201, R-205 |

### 8.3 Step 3's acceptance tests and evidence (v0.4, 2026-11-25) — registered before each run in the executor's registration record (`L7_step3_registration.txt`, amendments 1 – 9), each comparison **run once, alone, with `--list-tests` checked first and the log kept and hashed**; the old records stand beside every re-run

**What is gated and what is only reported.** A criterion below is a criterion; a number marked *reported* is printed beside its context and gates nothing (plan §4 rule 7: a prediction is not a criterion). **No default of `EST-R-306` was tuned to a result seen**; where a default changed, the row says by whose ruling and that it was registered before the re-run. The step's **evidence is not a claim that the scaled algorithm out-reaches `DTU`'s unscaled one** (`EST-R-309`).

| id | what is checked | expected value | source | tolerance | discharges |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `EST-A-300` | **the fixture, before any fit**: the 27 NIST nonlinear models written in `tests/nist_nls.hpp` (templates over the number type, evaluated on a forward-mode `Dual` for the Jacobian) are the files' models: (a) the counts of observations and parameters parsed equal the files' declared ones; (b) at the certified parameters the residual sum of squares equals NIST's certified one (relative `10⁻⁶`: eleven certified figures, sums of up to 250 terms); (c) the dual-number Jacobian equals a central difference of the double model at start 1 and at the certified point, column by column, to `10⁻⁶` of the column's norm | the certified sums of squares | `NISTN` | as stated; **the absolute floor `10⁻¹⁸` for Lanczos1** (a certified sum of `1.4 × 10⁻²⁵`, below the rounding of eleven-figure parameters) | — *(fixture)* |
| `EST-A-301` | **Levenberg–Marquardt on the 27 NIST nonlinear datasets**, from start 1, start 2 and the certified point (**81 runs**), with two controls computed in the test (plain Gauss–Newton through step 2's solve; `DTU`'s Algorithm 3.16 **unscaled**, Cholesky of `JᵀJ + μI`, τ = 10⁻³): **G1** from the certified point the least LRE over the parameters is `≥ 4` for all 27 (a status other than `converged_*` there is *listed* by name, not gated); **G2 the status never lies**: any run reporting `converged_*` either matches the certified values to LRE `≥ 4` **or** stands at a different stationary point with a larger objective, shown by an **independent** gradient (central differences of the double model, cosine `≤ 10⁻⁶`), listed by name; **G3** an objective smaller than NIST's beyond its printed precision (`10⁻¹⁰` relative) plus the absolute rounding floor is a *finding* (the test fails and says which); **G4 the control has power**: Gauss–Newton does not reach `F ≤ F_NIST(1 + 10⁻⁶)` from at least one of the 54 starts | gated: G1 – G4. *Reported (unpredicted):* the scaled library reaches NIST's `F` from **46 of 54** starts under `residual_fraction = 10⁻²⁴` (**47** under the first registration, `10⁻³⁰`: one run, Lanczos1 from start 2, now stops at `converged_residual` with `F` 11 times NIST's `7.15 × 10⁻²⁶`, at LRE 8.13); **The sail arc (tests in `tests/l7_arc/`, ids `ARC-*`; the force model, the measurement model and the data are in `tests/l7_arc/arc_fixture.hpp` and `arc_model.hpp`, whose headers are the specification of what the arc is).** A LightSail-2-like sail (32 m² on 4.93 kg, a flat sun-pointing macromodel, gravity to degree 10, the Sun, the Moon and SRP with the conical shadow, **drag off**, no `C_R` estimated; only the six state components at the first epoch are fitted), three days from UTC 2025-12-20, ranged by three SLRF2020 stations through L6's range model (σ = 0.01 m, one observation per 60 s per station above 10° elevation). **The same model generates and fits the data**: the arc tests the estimator's convergence on the full chain, not the model's fidelity. The no-eclipse arc (a dawn-dusk sun-synchronous plane; penumbra clearance +3.695° and `ν = 1` at all 25 920 samples at 10 s) and an eclipsing arc (i = 24°, **two** working stations: Herstmonceux never sees it; recorded, not gated). Starts: ladders **P** (along-track phase and semi-major axis: `s` km and `10 s` m) and **C** (the Cartesian direction of the first design), five rungs each, frozen from **model calls only** after the first design's ladder was found **not evaluable** (a start of `s ≥ 3` was refused by `MEAS-F-010` before a step, and the author's account of why was wrong until a model call measured it).

### 8.4 Step 4's acceptance tests — **registered 2026-11-27 (v0.5), before any line of step 4 exists** (plan §4 rules 3, 5 and 7)

**What "registered" means here.** The checks below are committed with their constructions, expected values, controls and predictions **before** `prior.hpp` or any line of the step is written; the module and its test files are then written and compiled **without running any group of them**; each group is then run **once, alone, with `--list-tests` checked first and the log kept and hashed** (the discipline of §8.2 and §8.3). **A figure marked *predicted* is a closed form computed here from the stated construction, not a result**; the test fails if it disagrees, and a disagreement is a finding about the module or about this arithmetic, reported as such, with the first record left standing. **Nothing here is a claim that a constraint helps** (`EST-R-407`). **What is gated and what is only reported** as in §8.3: a number marked *reported* gates nothing.

**Sources.** `BRN52` §7.5 and `GEO1` §10.1 – 10.2 print the formulas the module implements and **no number to reproduce** (§2; `PROVENANCE.md` §40.10), so the acceptance is by **identities**, each checked against an **independent** computation — the stacked whitened system solved by Householder QR in `long double` (a different algorithm from the normal equations and a wider format), an exact integer case, closed forms — and by **controls that must fail**. **The common fixture** (written in the tests' header before any run): a 64-bit splitmix generator seeded **20261127**; the RTN basis built in the test by the formula of `FRAME-R-040` (`ê_R = r/|r|`, `ê_N = (r × v)/|r × v|`, `ê_T = ê_N × ê_R`, a plain-double computation that does not link `frames`) for the **a priori state `r_A = (6878.137, 0, 0)` km, `v_A = (0, 5.381, 5.381)` km/s** (a near-circular orbit at 500 km altitude inclined 45°); for it **`B = [[1, 0, 0], [0, √½, √½], [0, −√½, √½]]`** and, a fact the controls use, **`B² = [[1, 0, 0], [0, 0, 1], [0, −1, 0]]` — a quarter turn about the radial axis, so that passing `Bᵀ` for `B` exchanges the weights of the transverse and normal axes exactly**.

| id | what is checked | expected value | source | tolerance | discharges |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `EST-A-401` | **the exact identity: the constrained solve is the stacked least-squares solution.** Problem **P1**: `n = 6` parameters, `m = 40` data rows (entries from the generator, columns scaled by `1, 10³, 10⁻³, 1, 1, 1` so that the scaling has work to do; `σₖ` from 0.5 to 2), an **isotropic** block on parameters 0 – 2 (`σ = 0.5, 2, 8`, centres offset by 1, −1, 0.5 from `x_lin`) and an **RTN** block on 3 – 5 (`B` of the fixture, `σ = (0.2, 200, 0.2)`, `d = (1, −1, 0.5)` inertial). (a) **the reference**: the stacked system `[A; L]c = [r̃; d̃]` (`m + r = 46` rows, `L` built in the test from the same definition of `Λ`) solved by Householder QR in `long double`; the solve's `x̂` agrees with it in the scaled error, **`φ(x̂, x_ref) ≤ B`** (the certified bound of `EST-R-206`, from the solve) plus the reference's own error, bounded by `46 · 2⁻⁶⁴ · κ_up`; (b) **an exact case**: `n = 3`, integer data, `σ` powers of two, solved in exact integer (fraction) arithmetic by Cramer's rule; equality within `B`; (c) the counts: `ν = m + r − n_free = 40`, `r = 6`, and **`Ω̂_data + Ω̂_prior`** equals the reference's `‖[A; L]c − [r̃; d̃]‖²` to `B`-relative; (d) **no prior is step 2's solve, bit for bit**: every field and the provenance string of a `NormalEquations` that never had `add_prior` called are `==` those of the same rows without step 4's header's existence — and **the whole of the existing estimation suite passes unmodified** (3258 assertions in 26 test cases at `tip-1928ac2`, plus the new ones); the new provenance keys `priors=2;prior_rows=6;` are present in P1's string and **absent** with no prior. **Controls (must fail, by ≥ 10³ B):** (i) the RTN block's `Λ` from the transposed basis; (ii) the isotropic block's centre offsets with the **sign reversed** (`x − x_A` for `x_A − x`); (iii) one `σ` perturbed by 10⁻⁶ relative | the stacked solution; the exact fractions | independent reference (QR, `long double`); exact arithmetic; `BRN52` §7.5, `GEO1` eq. 10.1-11 for what is being equalled | `φ ≤ B`; exact counts; `==` for (d); the controls' `φ ≥ 10³ B` | R-401, R-402, R-403 |
| `EST-A-402` | **the two limits of `σ`, and the exact marginal.** Problem **P2**: `n = 4`, `m = 30`, one isotropic block on parameter 0 (centre offset `d₀`), the data information such that `Q₀₀ = [N⁻¹]₀₀` (the unconstrained marginal variance, from the unconstrained solve) is read from the solve; `σ` is written as `k·√Q₀₀`. **Identity (exact for any `σ`, by the Schur complement):** `x₀(σ) − d₀ = (x₀(∞) − d₀) · σ² / (σ² + Q₀₀)`, **checked at `k = 10⁻¹², 10⁻⁶, 10⁻³, 10⁻², 10⁻¹, 1, 10, 10², 10⁶, 10¹²`**. **σ → ∞:** at `k = 10¹²` the solution is the unconstrained one within `B_c + B_u` (**predicted** deviation `≈ 10⁻²⁴` relative, far below `B`). **σ → 0:** at `k = 10⁻⁶` and `10⁻¹²` the solution is the **elimination of `EST-R-209`** at the centre (parameter 0 held at `x_lin₀ + d₀`) within `B_c + B_e`, **and the deviation at `k = 10⁻³, 10⁻², 10⁻¹` follows `σ²/(σ² + Q₀₀)` — the ratio of successive decades `≈ 100` (between 90 and 101 for `k ≤ 10⁻²`)**: the `O(σ²)` law, measured. *Predicted and reported, not gated:* `B_c` at `k = 10⁻¹²` is `≤ 10 B_e` (the information form scaled by powers of two keeps the conditioning of the eliminated problem); **gated:** `B_c < ½` (no refusal) at every `k`. **Control (must fail):** the same identity with `σ²/(σ² − Q₀₀)` or with the wrong sign of `d₀` is violated by `≥ 10³ B` at `k = 1` | the closed form; the elimination | `GEO1` §10.2 (the prior alone makes `A` invertible); `EST-R-209`; the Schur complement | `(B_c + B_u)(‖Dx̂_c‖ + ‖Dx̂_u‖)` in the scaled component; the decade ratio as stated | R-405 |

With `Q_data = N⁻¹` (from the unconstrained solve) and `Q_post` (from the constrained one): (a) **`Q_post⁻¹ − N = Λ`**: `(N + Λ)` formed in `long double` from the formed system and the test's own `Λ` equals the inverse of the reported cofactor within `4 B ‖N + Λ‖`; (b) **`Q_post ⪯ Q_data`**: `vᵀ(Q_data − Q_post)v ≥ −B vᵀQ_data v` for **10⁴ seeded unit directions `v`** in the scaled variables, the five axes and the weakest direction; and `tr Q_data − tr Q_post > 0`; (c) **`Q_post ⪯ Λ⁻¹`**: `vᵀ(Λ⁻¹ − Q_post)v ≥ −B vᵀΛ⁻¹v` for the same directions, `Λ⁻¹ = diag(σ²) ⊕ Bᵀdiag(σ²)B` from the test's formula. *(The order of the two information matrices: `Q_post⁻¹ − Q_data⁻¹ = Λ ⪰ 0`; the first-written text of the order had the difference the other way round and is corrected here.)* **Control (must fail):** the same checks with `N − Λ` (a *negative* information, built in the test's own dense code, not through the API) violate (b) at `≥ 10³ B` on at least one direction — the check can detect a wrong sign | the identity `Q_post⁻¹ = N + Λ` | `BRN52` §7.5; `GEO1` eq. 10.1-7 (`V = [BᵀΣ_z⁻¹B + Σ_A⁻¹]⁻¹`) | as stated | R-403 |

Start `(20, 4)`. the data are three rows that measure the **RTN components** of the true error `e` (row `k` is `B_k`) with standard deviations **`σ_R = σ_N = 0.01 m` and `σ_T = 10 m`** (tracking that fixes radial and normal and barely the along-track component); **(R)** `(−1.24688 × 10⁻⁴, −0.249377, −1.24688 × 10⁻⁴) m`; the ratio of the along-track biases **`400.840`** (the registered bar is `> 10`); posterior standard deviations (R, T, N): (I) `(0.00998753, 0.199960, 0.00998753)`, (R) `(0.00998753, 9.98752, 0.00998753)` m. **Gated** (noise-free, the solve's own answer in RTN components): each predicted error to `10⁻⁸` relative (values below `10⁻⁶ m`: `10⁻¹⁰ m` absolute); (ii) RTN `(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)` equals the isotropic solve to `B` (**frame-independence of an equal-`σ` block**) — and *that equality is itself gated*; (iii) RTN `(0.2, 0.2, 200)` gives `(−1.24688 × 10⁻⁴, −99.9600, −1.25 × 10⁻¹⁰) m` (**predicted**: the normal component loosened, the along-track protected). **Reported, not gated (`EST-R-407` iii):** **1000 replicates** with noise drawn from the data's own `σ` (the generator, seed 20261127, the polar form): the mean error per axis for (I) and (R) beside the prediction and the standard error of the mean — **predicted standard errors `(3.1583 × 10⁻⁴, 0.0063232, 3.1583 × 10⁻⁴)` m for (I) and `(3.1583 × 10⁻⁴, 0.31583, 3.1583 × 10⁻⁴)` m for (R)**: so the replicates **resolve the isotropic along-track bias at about 15 800 standard errors and cannot resolve the RTN one (0.79 standard errors)**, nor either radial or normal bias (0.39): **the evidence of this row is the closed form and the identity of `EST-A-401`, not the replicates**, and the table says so. **The measured power, stated before the run:** the comparison discriminates the two priors by a factor 400.84 in the along-track bias and the transposed-basis control by 400 (`99.96 / 0.2494`); neither number is a claim about any real orbit (`EST-R-407` ii) | the closed forms above | the construction; `GEO1` eq. 10.1-11; `BRN52` eq. 7.8 | as stated | R-407 |

*Fixed before any run, in the test files' headers (executor-classified, with the evidence of §3.7 and the arithmetic above):* the seed 20261127; the state `(r_A, v_A)`; the constants of `EST-A-405`'s data; the decade list of `EST-A-402`. **Not registered here, because nothing yet exists to measure them:** the figures of the budget and of the mutation testing (`EST-R-309`'s analogue for step 4 is the sandboxed harness of the closing sentence, written when the implementation exists).

**Results of the registered single run (2026-11-27, TREE-06; each case alone and once, `--list-tests` checked first — 7 cases in `odl_estimation_tests` and 1 in `odl_l7_prior_tests`; logs and their SHA-256 in `PROVENANCE.md` §40.10; the criteria above are untouched, nothing was tuned).** **5 of the 8 cases passed; 3 missed one registered criterion each, reported as written:**

| case | outcome | what the log shows (copied from the saved logs) |
|---|---|---|
| run 1 of `EST-A-401` | **passed** (269 assertions) | (a) `φ = 2.595 × 10⁻¹⁶` against `B = 7.943 × 10⁻¹³` (reference's own bound `2.206 × 10⁻¹⁷`, `κ_up = 8.845`); (b) the exact integer case `φ = 1.086 × 10⁻¹⁶` against `B = 2.035 × 10⁻¹³`; (c) `ν = 40`, `r = 6`, `Ω̂_data = 59.6121891221`, `Ω̂_prior = 5.85235763074`, total `65.4645467528` = the reference's `65.4645467528`; (d) no prior: every compared field `==`, the provenance literal as `EST-R-212`; controls: transposed basis `φ = 0.1767` (`B = 1.032 × 10⁻¹²`, ratio `1.7 × 10¹¹`), reversed centres `φ = 0.1913` (ratio `2.3 × 10¹¹`), one `σ` by 10⁻⁶ `φ = 9.119 × 10⁻⁸` (ratio `1.1 × 10⁵`) |
| run 1 of `EST-A-402` | **missed 1 of 516 assertions** | the identity held at all ten `k` (scaled errors `≤ 6.7 × 10⁻¹³` against tolerances `≥ 6.2 × 10⁻¹²`); `k = 10¹²`: `φ` to the unconstrained solution `0`; `k = 10⁻¹²`: `φ` to the elimination `1.273 × 10⁻¹⁶` (`B_c = 4.610 × 10⁻¹³`, `B_e = 4.186 × 10⁻¹³`), `B_c / B_e = 1.101` (predicted `≤ 10`); decade ratios `99.0198` and `99.9901` (registered 90 – 101); controls violated by `1.556` and `0.9661` (`B_c ≈ 5 × 10⁻¹³`). **The miss: at `k = 10⁻⁶`, `φ` to the elimination `= 9.029 × 10⁻¹³` against the registered bound `B_c + B_e = 8.797 × 10⁻¹³`.** The same run shows `x₀ = 1.99999999999824`, i.e. the deviation `1.76 × 10⁻¹²` from the centre that the registered law `Δ·σ²/(σ² + Q₀₀)` predicts at `k = 10⁻⁶`: the registered law and the registered "within `B_c + B_e` at `k = 10⁻⁶`" predict deviations of the same size, `10⁻¹²`, and the second was written without that comparison (observation from the log; no further investigation was made) |
| run 1 of `EST-A-403` | **passed** (680 assertions) | (a) ten `δ`: worst relative difference `3.467 × 10⁻¹⁶` (bound `10⁻¹³`); (b) four cases (RTN and isotropic, rotation and reflection): relative differences `1.008 × 10⁻¹⁵`, `1.782 × 10⁻¹⁶`, `2.387 × 10⁻¹⁶`, `2.446 × 10⁻¹⁶` against tolerances `5.4 × 10⁻¹³ – 1.3 × 10⁻¹²`; the transposed-basis control differs by `0.2067` and `0.05013` (`≥ 10³ B` required); (c) the bits: equal or scaled exactly for `p = (3, −2, 5)` and for `p ∈ {−7, 4, 11}`; *reported:* one RTN component scaled by 2 alone changes the solution by `0.2365` relative |
| run 1 of `EST-A-403 (d)`, case `403d` | **passed** (38 assertions) | the fixture and ten seeded states: the worst element difference between `frames::rtn_basis` and the `FRAME-R-040` formula `2.220 × 10⁻¹⁶` (bound `4u = 4.441 × 10⁻¹⁶`); a rectilinear state and a zero velocity refused with `FRAME-F-004`; every basis accepted by `PriorBlock::rtn` |
| run 1 of `EST-A-404` | **passed** (20 084 assertions) | (a) `Q_post⁻¹ = N + Λ` to `1.502 × 10⁻¹⁶` (tolerance `4 B_c = 2.056 × 10⁻¹²`); (b) the smallest margin `(Q_data − Q_post)/Q_data` over 10 006 directions `+1.687 × 10⁻⁴` (bound `≥ −9.781 × 10⁻¹³`), `tr Q_data − tr Q_post = 9.399 × 10⁻⁵`; (c) the smallest `(Λ⁻¹ − Q_post)/Λ⁻¹` `+0.9655`; control `N − Λ`: smallest margin `−0.03801` (required `≤ −9.781 × 10⁻¹⁰`) |
| run 1 of `EST-A-405` | **missed 1 of 29 assertions** (and the registered premise failed) | status `converged_gradient` in 7 passes (7 accepted, 0 rejected), `x = (2.00135900066, 0.505599724523)`, objective `0.265521703656601` against the independent `F_tot` to `1.034 × 10⁻¹⁴`; per-pass worst `1.112 × 10⁻¹⁴` over 7 passes; independent finite-difference cosine `1.726 × 10⁻¹¹` (bound `10⁻⁶`); `final_system` cofactor against `(N + Λ)⁻¹` `2.492 × 10⁻¹⁶`; `EST-F-404` returned and `refused` by LM. **The miss: the control's relative difference at the start `(20, 4)` is `1.797 × 10⁻⁴` (recorded `4151571.31`, independent `F_tot = 4152317.31`) against the registered `≥ 10⁻³`**: the data's half dominates `F_tot` there, the prior's half is `746.0`. **The registered premise (reported, not gated) FAILED: without the prior, LM from `(20, 4)` ended `converged_gradient` in 7 passes, 7 accepted, 0 rejected** — the problem is not weakly observable enough to fail without the prior (a finding, as `ARC-L-001` was) |

| run 1 of `EST-A-407` | **missed 1 of 12 052 assertions** | isotropic errors `(−1.246882793 × 10⁻⁴, −99.96001599, −1.246882793 × 10⁻⁴)` m — equal to the closed form to the digits printed; RTN `(−1.246882793 × 10⁻⁴, −0.2493765508, −1.246882793 × 10⁻⁴)` m against the predicted along-track `−0.2493765586`; the ratio `400.839677` (predicted `400.840`, bar `> 10`); T-axis posterior standard deviations `0.199960012` and `9.98752339` (predicted equal); controls: `Bᵀ` passed for `B` gives `−99.96001599` (factor `400.839` against the RTN prediction), RTN `(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)` equals the isotropic solve exactly (`0` difference, `B = 4.864 × 10⁻¹²`), RTN `(0.2, 0.2, 200)` gives `(−1.246882793 × 10⁻⁴, −99.96001599, −1.249986781 × 10⁻¹⁰)` m. **The miss: the RTN along-track error `−0.2493765508` against `−0.2493765586` is a relative difference `3.1 × 10⁻⁸`, the registered gate was `10⁻⁸`** (an absolute difference of `7.8 × 10⁻⁹` m on an estimate of about 99.75 m, `7.8 × 10⁻¹¹` relative to it; the certified `B` of this solve was not part of the registered tolerance). *Reported replicates (1000, seed 20261127):* isotropic along-track mean error `−99.95979` (`15 808` standard errors from zero, predicted `−99.96002`); RTN `+0.3046` (predicted `−0.2494`, standard error `0.3158`: `0.79` standard errors, **not resolved, as registered**); radial and normal biases not resolved (`0.39` standard errors) |

**What the three misses are, said once.** None was changed after the run: the test code implements the criteria as registered, the tolerance of `EST-A-402` at `k = 10⁻⁶`, the control's `10⁻³` of `EST-A-405` and the `10⁻⁸` of `EST-A-407` stand as written, and the `EST-A-405` premise stands as a failed premise. The step's other 33 860 assertions (of 33 863) held. Whether the three are to be re-registered (a new, dated row beside each, the first kept) or the implementation is to be revised is the manager's call; step 4 is **not** closed (`L7-4.md` stays `TODO`).

**Amendment 1 of step 4's gate (2026-11-27, by `odl Committee`, after run 1; the first-registered rows and criteria above stand as written and the run-1 logs are kept — the three cases below are re-run once as run 2, the other five are not re-run).** The first texts are *not* deleted or overwritten; each amended criterion is stated beside the first, which run 1 missed as reported above.
- **`EST-A-402`.** First text: *"at `k = 10⁻⁶` and `10⁻¹²` the solution is the elimination of `EST-R-209` at the centre within `B_c + B_e`"*. **Amended:** the direct agreement with the exact elimination is **gated at `k = 10⁻¹²` only** (the Schur gap `O(k²) ≈ 10⁻²⁴` is far below `B_c + B_e`); at **`k = 10⁻⁶`** the Schur gap itself is `1.76 × 10⁻¹²`, larger than `B_c + B_e = 8.797 × 10⁻¹³`, so the solution is gated against the **exact Schur-complement prediction** — `x₀ = d₀ + (x₀(∞) − d₀)·σ²/(σ² + Q₀₀)` (already gated at every `k`) and, for the free parameters, the rank-one update `xᵢ = xᵢ(∞) + Qᵢ₀/Q₀₀ · (x₀ − x₀(∞))` with `Q` the unconstrained cofactor — within `B_c + B_e`. The elimination agreement at `k = 10⁻⁶` is still printed, not gated.
- **`EST-A-405`.** The **registered premise** (reported, not gated) — *without the prior, LM from `(20, 4)` does not end `converged_gradient`* — **stays recorded as a failed premise, a finding** (without the prior the run ended `converged_gradient` in 7 passes, 7 accepted, 0 rejected); neither the start nor the model is adjusted. **The control's relative-difference threshold is amended from `10⁻³` to `10⁻⁴`**: at the start `(20, 4)` the prior's half of `F_tot` is `746.0` of `4.15 × 10⁶`, i.e. `1.797 × 10⁻⁴`, about `10¹⁰` times the `10⁻¹⁴` agreement of the real objective with the independent `F_tot`.
- **`EST-A-407`.** The relative tolerance on the **RTN along-track error** (`c_T − 100 m`, a subtraction of `100 m` from an estimate of `99.75 m`, 2.6 digits of cancellation under the solve's certified `B ≈ 5 × 10⁻¹²`) is amended from `10⁻⁸` to **`10⁻⁶`**. The other axes keep `10⁻⁸`; the primary criterion, the ratio of the along-track biases `> 10` (measured in run 1: `400.839677`, predicted `400.840`), and every control are unchanged.
- **Two clarifications, confirmed by the Committee (they change no number):** `EST-R-403` computes `B` over **all `m + r` rows** (data and prior; see the note added to `EST-R-403`), and `EST-A-403 (c)` scales the prior's `σ` and centre by **`2^(−p)`** when a data column is multiplied by `2^p` — the reading consistent with `EST-R-211` (the parameter is then `2^(−p)` of what it was); §8.4's "by the same `2^(p)`" is to be read that way.

**Run 2 (2026-11-27, TREE-07: the three amended cases only, each once, `--list-tests` checked first; no code of step 4 changed, run 1's logs are kept; logs and hashes in `PROVENANCE.md` §40.10).** **All three passed under the amended criteria.** `EST-A-402` (516 assertions): at `k = 10⁻¹²` `φ` to the elimination `1.273 × 10⁻¹⁶` against `B_c + B_e = 8.797 × 10⁻¹³`; at `k = 10⁻⁶` `φ` to the Schur prediction `3.088 × 10⁻¹⁶` against `8.797 × 10⁻¹³` (the elimination agreement there, printed and not gated, is again `9.029 × 10⁻¹³`, the Schur gap). `EST-A-405` (29 assertions): the control's relative difference `1.797 × 10⁻⁴` against the amended `10⁻⁴`, everything else as in run 1; **the premise failed again, as a finding** (without the prior `converged_gradient` in 7 passes, 7 accepted, 0 rejected). `EST-A-407` (12 052 assertions): the RTN along-track error `−0.2493765508` against `−0.2493765586` within the amended `10⁻⁶`; the ratio of the along-track biases **`400.839677`** (predicted `400.840`, bar `> 10`) as in run 1. *(The printed label of the `EST-A-405` control still reads "must be >= 1e-3": the text of that line was not changed; the assertion is `10⁻⁴`.)*

**`PREP-04` — sandboxed mutation testing of step 4's code, and the `M10` guard (2026-11-27, TREE-10; the first texts above stand).** `odl-prep`'s `PREP-04` ran the sandboxed mutants of the step-4 constraint implementation on `tip-1f45eda`: **9 of 10 killed**. The survivor, **`M10`** (`normal_equations.cpp:234`: `d = block.centre[j] − linearisation_point[at[j]]` mutated to `block.centre[j]`), survived because **every `add_prior` call of `EST-A-401` – `-407` passed a zero linearisation point**. At the Committee's order a targeted case was **added to `EST-A-401`**: the prior is added at a **nonzero** `x_lin` with `x_A ≠ x_lin`, and the solved correction must equal the exact shifted system `N δx = b + Λ(x_A − x_lin)` (the stacked QR reference built with the same `x_lin`) within `B` plus the reference bound; the control — the same block added with `x_lin = 0`, which is what the mutant computes — must differ from the shifted reference by `≥ 10³ B`. **The guard is pending re-verification** (`PREP-05` kills `M10` in the sandbox); the figures it measures are in `PROVENANCE.md` §40.10. *(Same lesson as step 3's `M3` / `M5`: a mutant a test cannot see is a test gap, not a code defect; no line of `normal_equations.cpp` changed.)*

**Closing evidence for step 4 (2026-11-27, TREE-12; every text above stands as written; the figures are copied from `PREP-05.REPORT.md` and `RUN-06.REPORT.md`).** **`PREP-05` re-verified `M10`** (`normal_equations.cpp:234`, `departure[j] = block.centre[j];`) on `tip-9081953` in the sandbox: **`KILLED`** — build exit 0, `odl_estimation_tests "EST-A-401*"` exit 42, by **`EST-A-401` at `prior_tests.cpp:441`**, `CHECK( phim <= bound )` → `0.23853279230081736 <= 0.00000000000079434` (the baseline `EST-A-401` on the unmutated sandbox: `All tests passed (361 assertions in 1 test case)`, exit 0). **With `PREP-04`'s nine, step 4's sandboxed mutation testing is now `10 of 10` mutants killed.** **`RUN-06`** (`odl-runner`, `tools/ci.sh --prove-offline` from the isolated working copy of `tip-9081953`, `CXX=g++-13`): **`== 14 of 14 gates passed ==`**, exit 0 — recorded below beside step 5, since the one run covers both steps. The guard `M10` was added to `EST-A-401` by TREE-10 (above); no line of `normal_equations.cpp` changed.

### 8.5 Step 5's acceptance tests — **registered 2026-11-27 (v0.6), before any line of step 5 exists** (plan §4 rules 3, 5 and 7) *(not built; Step 5 specification registration before code — the convention of `EST-A-900`: `speccheck` reads the rows, which discharge `EST-R-501` – `-507` and `EST-F-501` – `-505`, and cannot tell whether they are implemented)*

**What "registered" means here**, as in §8.2 – §8.4: the constructions, constants, tolerances and controls below are committed **before** `joint_covariance.hpp` or any line of the step is written; the code and its tests are then written and compiled **without running any group**; each group is then run **once, alone, with `--list-tests` checked first and the log kept and hashed**. A constant marked *predicted* is a closed form computed here from the stated construction (with `bc -l`, 30 digits), not a result. **If a registered criterion is missed the run reports it as written; the first text is never overwritten**; an amendment is the manager's, dated, beside the first (as `EST-A-402` / `-405` / `-407` were, Amendment 1). **The test-level tolerance rule, stated first because step 4 taught it:** a tolerance on a quantity that comes out of a solve is **derived from that solve's certified `B`** (`EST-R-206`), not written as a bare small number; where this section writes `10⁻¹¹` or `10⁻¹³` it is a *floor on top of* `B`, said at the place.

**Sources of the references.** Nothing prints a worked joint region of a fit (§2, `PROVENANCE.md` §40.11), so every reference is **computed independently of the module under test**: closed forms with `bc -l`; the CDFs of `EHB` as the round trip; **Householder QR in `long double` on the rows** for every objective value; an independent re-fit for the difference variance; `long double` Gauss–Jordan inverses. **Fixture:** the NIST linear sets as the doubles of `tests/fixture.hpp` (`Norris` n = 2, `Pontius` n = 3, `Longley` n = 7; read from the pinned cache); the seed **20261127** (splitmix64, as §8.4).

| id | what is checked | expected value | source | tolerance | discharges |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `EST-A-501` | *(not built when registered; built 2026-11-27, TREE-09 — results below)* **the critical values, against independent constants and their own CDFs; the edges; the refusals.** (a) `chi2_2_critical(p)` at `p = 0.95, 0.99, 0.682689492137086, −expm1(−½)`; (b) `two_f_2_nu_critical(p, ν)` and `F = ½·(it)` at `(ν, p) = (1, 0.99), (3, 0.99), (10, 0.95), (100, 0.95)`; (c) **the CDF round trip** (the test's own route): `1 − e^(−c²/2)` returns `p`, and `1 − (1 + c²/ν)^(−ν/2)` returns `p` (with `x = c²/2` in `1 − (1 + 2x/ν)^(−ν/2)`); (d) **edges and far outside:** `p = 10⁻³⁰⁰` (`c² = 2 × 10⁻³⁰⁰`), `p = 1 − 2⁻⁵³` through the `p` form and **`α = 2⁻⁵³` through the tail form** (`73.4736011393542…`), `ν = 1`, and `ν = 10⁹` (`2F` within `10⁻⁷` of `χ²₂(0.95)`); (e) the refusals `EST-F-501`: `p ∈ {0, 1, −0.1, 1.1, NaN, ±∞}`, `α` likewise, `ν ∈ {0, −1}`. **Controls (must fail by ≥ 10⁻³ relative):** `−ln(1 − p)` (the factor 2 missing) and `F` where `2F` is meant | **predicted:** `χ²₂(0.95) = 5.991464547107982`, `χ²₂(0.99) = 9.210340371976183`, `χ²₂(0.682689492137086) = 2.295748928898637`, `χ²₂(1 − e^(−½)) = 1` (**the 39.35 % contour; `AND10` §2.5.2 prints 39.4 %, `\|0.393469 − 0.394\| ≤ 5 × 10⁻⁴`**); `2F`: `(1, 0.99) → 9999` (`F = 4999.5`; **the e-Handbook prints 4999.52, a slip of the table: reported**), `(3, 0.99) → 61.63304070095651` (`F = 30.81652035047826`; **the table prints 30.816: reported**), `(10, 0.95) → 8.205642030260803` (`F = 4.102821015130401`; the handbook's 3-decimal `4.103` as the order states, **not re-read, the pages not being pinned**), `(100, 0.95) → 6.174591785497862` (`F = 3.087295892748931`; `3.087`); `ν = 10⁹`: `5.9914645650568` (relative `3.0 × 10⁻⁹` from `χ²₂`) | `EHB` closed forms (derivations); `AND10` §2.5.2; the CDFs | `8u` relative against the constants (`u = 2⁻⁵³`); the round trip `8u` relative in `p`; edges as stated; refusals exact; controls `≥ 10⁻³` | R-501, R-507, F-501 |

a `ForceSet` of a two-body force (`μ = 3.986004415 × 10⁵ km³/s²`) and three constant-acceleration forces along `x, y, z` of magnitude `10⁻⁹ km/s²` each scaled by its parameter (re-implemented in the test file against the L3 `Force` interface, as `modules/dynamics/tests/sensitivity_tests.cpp` does), all parameter values `1`; the initial state `r = (7000, 0, 0) km`, `v = (0, √(μ/7000), 0) km/s` (a circular orbit of period `5828.5 s`); **58 epochs, every 100 s from 100 s to 5800 s**, at each `propagate_with_sensitivities` giving `Φ` and the sensitivity columns **by identity** (`column(reg, id)`); the observations are the three position components, `σ = 10⁻³ km`, noise `0.8 σ` times seeded normals (seed 20261127, linearised at the truth: the residual is the noise); rows over the **9 parameters** `(x, y, z, vx, vy, vz, c0, c1, c2)`. **Gated:** (a) the layout `create(state names, names from the registry's declarations in issue order)` equals `Solution::names`; `DYN-R-005` | as stated | R-503, R-504, F-505 |

**Two readings I made where §8.5 was ambiguous (executor-classified, in the test header):** `EST-A-504 (a)` — the angle is checked bit-identical for a **common** power of two only (for `p_i ≠ p_j` the ellipse is deformed and its orientation changes; the critical value and `quadratic` are checked for both); `EST-A-504 (b)` — `JointCovariance` is filled by hand from the `Solution`, because the NIST sets have no six-parameter state (`joint_covariance()` is exercised by `EST-A-506` and the `EST-F-505` cases of `EST-A-505`). **What the six misses are, said once.** None was changed after the run: the test code implements the criteria as registered. The first (`0.394`) and the last three (the control's power) are registered arithmetic or predictions that the numbers do not bear out; the two `8u` misses are the reference evaluated at the exact `0.99` where the module evaluates at the double `0.99`. The module's own results agree with every independent reference in the log. Whether the two cases are to be re-registered (dated rows beside the first, as for step 4's Amendment 1) is the manager's call; step 5 is **not** closed (`L7-5.md` stays `TODO`).

**Amendment 1 of step 5's gate (2026-11-27, by `odl Committee`, after run 1; the first-registered rows and criteria above stand as written and run 1's logs are kept — the two cases below are re-run once as run 2, the other four are not re-run).**
- **`EST-A-501`.** (a) *First text:* `|p − 0.394| ≤ 5 × 10⁻⁴` for `p = 1 − e^(−½)` (`AND10`'s printed 39.4 %). **Amended:** `≤ 6 × 10⁻⁴` (the exact difference is `5.3066 × 10⁻⁴`); the exact check of `χ²₂(1 − e^(−½))` against 1 at `8u` is **kept**. (b) *First text:* `2F` against the `bc` constants at `8u` everywhere. **Amended:** at `p = 0.99` (`ν = 1, 3`; the exact decimal `99/100` constants `9999` and `61.633040700956512`) the tolerance is **`64u`** (`7.1 × 10⁻¹⁵`: `1/(1 − p) = 100` amplifies the representation rounding of `double(0.99)`); **`8u` is kept** on `p = 0.95` and on the tail form `two_f_2_nu_critical_from_tail(0.01, ν)` (added as assertions at `8u`).
- **`EST-A-504`.** (a) **Recorded:** a bit-identical ellipse orientation holds for a **common** power of two (`p_i = p_j`); an unequal `p_i ≠ p_j` deforms the ellipse and changes the angle (the critical value and `quadratic` stay bit-identical for both). (b) *First text:* the sum-formula control differs from the difference formula by `≥ 10⁻³` whenever `|ρ| ≥ 0.5`. **Amended:** where the standard deviations differ by orders of magnitude (`min SD / max SD < 10⁻²`, Pontius's twelve orders) `C_ii ≫ C_jj` bounds the relative difference to about `|2C_ij|/(C_ii + C_jj)`, so the required minimum is **`≥ 0.5·|2C_ij|/(C_ii + C_jj)`**; **`≥ 10⁻³` is kept** where `min SD / max SD ≥ 10⁻²` and `|ρ| ≥ 0.5`. The 14 exact difference-sd checks (`≤ 2.17 × 10⁻¹⁴`) and the 32 invariance cases are unchanged.
- **Recorded:** `JointCovariance::eliminated` (§5.4, note added there).

**Run 2 (2026-11-27, TREE-10: the two amended cases only, each once, `--list-tests` checked first; no code of steps 2 – 5 changed, run 1's logs are kept; logs and hashes in `PROVENANCE.md` §40.11).** **`EST-A-504` passed** (2 364 assertions): the 32 invariance cases and the 14 difference-sd checks as in run 1, and the amended control held on all four pairs below the SD ratio `10⁻²` — `(Norris 0, 1)` ratio `1.846 × 10⁻³`, required `1.429 × 10⁻³`, measured `2.853 × 10⁻³`; `(Pontius 0, 1)` `1.462 × 10⁻⁶`, `1.300 × 10⁻⁶`, `2.599 × 10⁻⁶`; `(0, 2)` `4.509 × 10⁻¹³`, `3.522 × 10⁻¹³`, `7.150 × 10⁻¹³`; `(1, 2)` `3.084 × 10⁻⁷`, `2.995 × 10⁻⁷`, `5.991 × 10⁻⁷`. **`EST-A-501` missed 1 of 93 assertions**: the `|p − 0.394|` comparison at `6 × 10⁻⁴` held, and `2F` at `p = 0.99` held at `64u`, but **the amended-in assertion on the tail form** `two_f_2_nu_critical_from_tail(0.01, 1)` against `9999` at the kept `8u = 8.88 × 10⁻¹⁶` measured `9.096 × 10⁻¹⁶` (the module returned `9998.9999999999909`; the `ν = 3` tail case, `6.610 × 10⁻¹⁶`, held). The amendment's stated reason for keeping `8u` there — that `double(0.01)` is almost exact — is true of the input, but `expm1` of an argument of `9.21` carries a relative error of about `9.21 u`, which is the size seen (observation; the same amplification is in the `p`-form misses of run 1). Nothing was changed after the run. The full `odl_estimation_tests` binary: **38 test cases, 37 passed, 1 failed (`EST-A-501`)**, 43 969 assertions, 43 968 passed; `odl_l7_prior_tests` and `odl_l7_joint_tests` exit 0; the ctest regex `…|estimation|prior|joint` is `100% tests passed … out of 21` (it does not select `EST-A-501`). **The `M10` guard in `EST-A-401`** (361 assertions, exit 0): with a nonzero `x_lin`, `φ = 3.454 × 10⁻¹⁶` against `B` + the reference bound `8.015 × 10⁻¹³`; the control (`x_lin = 0`, what the mutant computes) differs from the shifted reference by `φ = 0.2385`, `3.0 × 10¹¹` times `B`. Whether the one assertion is to be amended again (a dated row beside the first two) is the manager's call; step 5 is **not** closed.

**Amendment 2 of step 5's gate (2026-11-27, by `odl Committee`, after run 2; every text above stands as written, run 1's and run 2's logs are kept).** *First text (Amendment 1):* `two_f_2_nu_critical_from_tail(0.01, ν)` against the decimal constants at `8u`. **Amended, for `ν = 1` only: `16u` (`1.78 × 10⁻¹⁵`)** — `expm1(y)` at `y = −2 ln(0.01) = 9.21034` has relative condition number about `9.21`, so one rounding of `ln(0.01)` becomes about `9.21 u` in the result (`ν = 3` has `y ≈ 3.07` and held `8u` at `6.61 × 10⁻¹⁶`); `8u` is **kept** for `ν = 3` and for `p = 0.95`. Only that one tolerance in `joint_covariance_tests.cpp` changed (and docs); no code of steps 2 – 5.
