## 9. Provenance obligations

At each step's close, `PROVENANCE.md` records: the step's rule-4 sources and absences with their searches; the rulings and their dates; every frozen sizing before the run it governs, with the outcome beside it; the pins made. The exposure of the predecessor's lines named above is entered in `PROVENANCE.md` §0.2. *(Step 2, v0.3:)* the rule-4 report's sources, absences and rulings, the exploratory computations and their disclosure as the premise of the controls, and the pins are in `PROVENANCE.md` §40.7; the frozen sizing is §6.2 and `tools/estimation_sizing.cpp` (then `.py`); each registered group's outcome is recorded beside its criterion, the criterion unchanged.

## 10. Open questions for the manager

| id | question | author's recommendation |
|---|---|---|
| `EST-Q-001` | **Which formula does the frozen `G-*` statistic carry** — the 3-D RMS over the epochs, a per-component RMS, a degrees-of-freedom-corrected one; which epochs; which denominator? The manager read the script at L4 step 7; this layer may not. | One line from the manager before the fits are built; until then §8.1's reading stands and its contingency is registered. **ANSWERED 2026-10-06 (v0.2), by the manager, from his own record:** 3-D (the printed "position RMS" squared equals the sum of the printed x / y / z RMS squared, to four digits), on the file's own 900 s sampling; the denominator, `N` or `N − 7`, is inside the fitting program and not shown by its output — **both are reported, and the three outcomes of §8.1's amendment are registered before any fit runs.** |
| `EST-Q-002` | Step 1's five rulings (plugin granularity and module; the two additive L2 entry points; L4's ranking relativity rows; the truncation criterion; the Jacobians' finite-difference gate), set out with recommendations in the rule-4 report. | As recommended there. **RULED 2026-10-06 (v0.2):** all five yes, with the manager's conditions — R1 the plugins in a module of their own (`forcemodel`); R2 the two entry points, checked by the field's own identities; R3 L4's ranking rows corrected as step 1's **first** commit, one band per term from sources independent of the corrected output, registered before the re-run, with a sweep of every consumer; R4 the criterion as proposed, RMS over the sphere with the orbit maximum beside it, a function and a table at L4's four points; R5 L6's form, with the comparator's own error, `ν` measured over the whole stencil and asserted point by point, the field's `F` a rigorous bound with its looseness stated, and every control's power written down before the run. F4 and F5 carried. Recorded at `PROVENANCE.md` §40.3 onward. |
| `EST-Q-003` | **R7's one hatch** (plan §3.11.5: each refusal ships with exactly one named, logged hatch): a truncated minimum-norm solution in the scaled space with an explicit cut-off, or elimination, or none at step 2. | I recommended the minimum-norm solution. **RULED 2026-10-06 (v0.3), against it:** the one hatch is **elimination of named parameters at stated values, logged** (`EST-R-209`) — the operation a hard constraint is — because a minimum-norm solution *returns numbers for combinations the data cannot see*, which is the very failure step 2 exists to stop. |
| `EST-Q-004` | May the NIST StRD files, the NIST handbook tables, `DEM89`, `DTU`, `NIE99`, `MINP`, `GEO1` and `AND10` be pinned, and on what licence basis? | **RULED yes, 2026-10-06:** URL and hash only, redistributing nothing; **no NIST data file is copied into the tree**; tests read the pinned cache; specifications and tests quote certified numbers as facts with the citation; the licence sentences are quoted in the manifest entries; `VDS69`'s scan is not pinned. (The handbook tables turned out to be not byte-stable and are not pinned: PROVENANCE §40.7.) |
| `EST-Q-005` | Third-party copies of copyrighted books that search results offered (Numerical Recipes, Montenbruck–Gill, Tapley–Schutz–Born). | **RULED 2026-10-06:** not opened — and the same rule is **applied to `VDS69`**: one course-page scan of the paywalled 1969 paper was read once; it is recorded as read and **NOT a source**, the theorem is cited only through `DEM89` and `LAPK`. Third-party copies of paywalled works are not used from here on. |
| `EST-Q-006` | Where the state is estimated for the exit gate's fits (the arc's middle epoch against its start). | **Decided with the exit gate's fit** (the manager, 2026-10-06): the middle epoch lowers the correlation between position and velocity — NIST's own remedy for Filip is centring — and costs nothing in the estimator, which is indifferent. |
| `EST-Q-007` | *(step 4, v0.5, 2026-11-27)* **How many degrees of freedom does a prior carry?** `EST-R-403` counts the prior's rows as observations, `ν = m + r − n_free`, and reports the data's and the prior's residual sums separately. `BRN52` §7.5 calls the constraints pseudo-observations but prints the degrees of freedom (§7.2, eq. 7.5, `f = n − u`) for the data alone; `GEO1` §10.1 – 10.2 prints no `σ̂₀²`. | **Registered as written** (the one reading consistent with the pseudo-observation form of `BRN52` §7.5, and the one under which `σ̂₀²` stays the right unbiased estimate when the prior is true); the data-only alternative `ν = m − n_free` is computable from the two residual sums the solution carries, so the choice changes no stored number. The executor proposed it. **RULED YES, as registered, by `odl Committee`, 2026-11-27 (TREE-06).** |
| `EST-Q-008` | *(step 4, v0.5, 2026-11-27)* **Where does `rtn_basis` live for step 4?** §5.3 keeps `estimation` `core`-only: the caller passes the nine doubles of `frames::rtn_basis` and a test links both modules. The alternative is a dependency of `estimation` on `frames`. | **Registered `core`-only** (step 4's evidence needs no state; a second `rtn_basis` would be a second copy of `FRAME-R-040`); `EST-A-403 (d)` compares the two bases element for element (the test is `EST-A-403 (d)`, case `403d`, `tests/l7_prior/prior_frames_tests.cpp`). The executor proposed it. **RULED YES, as registered, by `odl Committee`, 2026-11-27 (TREE-06).** |
| `EST-Q-009` | *(step 5, v0.6, 2026-11-27)* **Where is the nonlinearity bound of an orbit fit's confidence region registered?** Ruling R15 asks the region of an orbit fit to be checked against the objective's grid profile "within a nonlinearity bound registered first", and the exit gate's finite-difference correlations. Step 5's own evidence is exact (linear NIST sets, `EST-A-503`) and the joint-covariance assembly (`EST-A-506`); it makes no claim about an orbit fit's region (`EST-R-506`). | **With the exit gate's fit** (§8.1), where the fit, its force set and its start exist and the bound can be derived from them before the run (the FD sizing frozen first, in step 1's form `ε(h) = h²F/6 + ν/h`). The executor proposed it. **RULED YES, as registered, by `odl Committee`, 2026-11-27 (TREE-09).** |
| `EST-Q-010` | *(exit gate, v0.7, 2026-11-27)* **How does the seventh parameter reach the registry?** `Srp` consumes no registered parameter (`PHPR-R-005`, ruled); the frozen `G-*` fits fit an SRP scale; `joint_covariance` needs it as a registered parameter in registry order. Options: (a) a decorator `ScaledForce` over any `dyn::Force` in `forcemodel` (`EST-R-901`: `a → c·a`, both Jacobians times `c`, `d(a)/d(c) = a_inner`), which changes **no** L4 code and serves the cannonball and the box-wing alike; (b) an optional `ParameterId` in `Srp` (reopens `PHPR-R-005`'s ruling and edits L4); (c) ECOM's `D0` as the seventh (a different physical parameter, not what the frozen fits fit). | **(a)**, in `modules/forcemodel/` (additive: `forcemodel` depends on `dyn`; step 1's code is not touched); `EST-A-903` is its gate. The executor proposes; **RULED (a) by `odl Committee`, 2026-11-27 (TREE-14): `ScaledForce` in `modules/forcemodel/`, no L4 change, gated by `EST-A-903`.** |
| `EST-Q-011` | *(exit gate, v0.7)* **The cannonball template's area and mass, and two tide options.** The frozen fits used "a template's single `area` and `mass`" (`oracle/ORACLE.md` §6); **no value is recorded anywhere in this tree** (`PROVENANCE.md` §40.12: the search). The fit's residual does not depend on them (only the product with `c` enters, a scale degeneracy), but the starting `c = 1` and the reading of `c·A·C_R/m` do. And §A.5 ruled the ocean tide's degree (7) but not whether Desai's ocean pole tide is in set A for the GPS fits. | **Mass:** the cited masses of `SPEC-spacecraft` (SVN63 `1633 kg`, SVN50 `1080 kg`); **area:** the box-wing macromodel's sun-facing projected area at zero off-sun angle for the same block, printed by the test; `C_R = 1` (a perfectly absorbing sphere); the fit repeated with the nominal doubled and halved **as a reported check** (`S_N` unchanged to the fit's tolerance). **Ocean pole tide: not in set A** (the smallest of the four tide terms, not in §A.5's set; its effect printed beside). The executor proposes; **RULED YES by `odl Committee`, 2026-11-27 (TREE-14), as recommended** (the cited masses 1633 / 1080 kg, the box-wing macromodel's sun-facing projected area at zero off-sun angle printed by the test, `C_R = 1`, the ×2 and ×0.5 checks reported; Desai's ocean pole tide **out** of set A, its effect printed beside). |

The executor proposes; **RULED YES (offline-clean) by `odl Committee`, 2026-11-27 (TREE-14):** the manifest was checked first — **the IGS satellite-metadata SINEX is not pinned** (only the SLR SINEX files are), so no network pin is added; the box-wing blocks are labelled "mapping G01=SVN63 (IIF) / G05=SVN50 (IIR-M) read 2026-09-24 (`PROVENANCE.md` §30.2); §30.1 'February 2023' noted stale against SP3 headers 2023-01-22/23" (`PROVENANCE.md` §40.12). |

The executor proposes; **RULED YES by `odl Committee`, 2026-11-27 (TREE-14), as recommended:** `EST-A-901` is gated for arcs with `ν = 1` throughout; a shadow-crossing arc (`ν < 1`) is printed and brought to the Committee, not gated on `EST-A-901`. |
